- Actor retained snapshots: 26 tickets, 7500 bytes. Players 8 within bound 8, 16 tickets, 5040 bytes. Companions count 2, bound 64, 3 tickets, 1755 bytes. Hostiles count 1, bound 64, 3 tickets, 315 bytes. Passives count 1, bound 32, 3 tickets, 312 bytes. Metadata 1 ticket, 78 bytes.

## Task 1.1c freeze inventory and bounds, 2026-09-27

Ruling: the constructor maxima measured by task 1.1b at `c6bb98719c8d6cc1fb53a898713e662c835fe8d0` are accepted. Node 1.2 may use these maxima. Queued commands stay 4096, ready chunk results stay 64, pending chunk saves stay 8 records, and encoded ownership stays 4194304 bytes. The proposed single `max_owned_snapshots` of 8 across every `SaveKey` does not fit the supported player schedule of 16 retained tickets, so it is replaced by per-lane caps: players 16, companions 3, hostiles 3, passives 3, metadata 1, and chunks 8. Workers stay 2 because 1.1b did not measure worker cardinality. Go-derived defaults stay 8 players, 512 outbox frames, 64 snapshot chunks, and 1 MiB snapshot publication. ## Task 1.2 compiling contract, 2026-09-27

Baseline before this edit was `87b01ca5f50cfaa33a236aa8beedb2565e5a6d3a`. The crate `packages/engine/crates/mornlea_server` now declares the checked server, save, and agent types from the execution and core-seam plans, keeps `AuthorityState` opaque, and registers the later topic modules empty of behavior. The executing consumer double is not server acceptance. No world rule, reducer, transport adapter, or disk backend was added. `HandshakeLimits` is not declared in the core-seam plan and was not invented.

A deliberately non-atomic compound stage applied the first inventory patch and then rejected the second. `contract_double::compound_rejects_partial` failed before the atomic stage was restored:

```
assertion `left == right` failed
  left: HotbarSlot(1)
 right: HotbarSlot(0)
```

The atomic stage validates every component before applying any, and the same test then passed with the selected slot still at 0. A refused save returns the original snapshots. A completed save echoes the exact key and revision.

Validation from the repository root, all exit 0: `rustup run 1.97.1 cargo metadata --manifest-path packages/engine/Cargo.toml --no-deps --format-version 1` lists `mornlea_server`. `rustup run 1.97.1 cargo test --manifest-path packages/engine/Cargo.toml -p mornlea_server --test server_contract --locked -- --list` lists 8 tests, including every required double. `rustup run 1.97.1 cargo test --manifest-path packages/engine/Cargo.toml -p mornlea_server --test server_contract --locked contract_double` passed 7 tests. The same binary's inventory row check also passed. `git diff --check` passed. Commit message: `feat(server): land compiling authoritative server contract`. Architecture skill: no change; this lands the change-local contract and does not add a new cross-task architecture rule.

## Task 1.2 review fixes, 2026-09-27

`TickContext::stage` counts projectile inserts already accepted in the same compound before it applies any component. A later apply failure restores the inventories, world, blocks, projectiles, and environment copied before that compound. A temporary path that neither counted those inserts nor restored the overlay failed `contract_double::compound_projectile_insert_is_atomic` with projectile length 128 against 127, while `contract_double::compound_rejects_partial` stayed green. The counted path then passed with length 127 and the selected slot still 0.

`AuthorityState::publish` converts each `RoutedEvent` with `ServerPacket::try_from(Event)` and encodes every control packet through `ProtocolCodec::encode_server_into`. A conversion or encode refusal returns `ServerError::InvalidInput` for field `packet` and appends no frame from that publication. `contract_double::event_publication_reaches_outbox` finds the encoded command-rejection event ahead of the 24-byte login frame.

Validation: `rustup run 1.97.1 cargo test --manifest-path packages/engine/Cargo.toml -p mornlea_server --test server_contract --locked contract_double` exited 0, 9 passed. `git diff --check` passed. Commit message: `fix(server): make contract staging and publication atomic`. Architecture skill: no change.

## Task 1.3 session admission and intake, 2026-09-28

Subagent execution (Superpowers implementer plus independent reviewer) over contract predecessor `10023cfc`. The provider module `mornlea_server/src/core/session.rs` now owns session lifecycle semantics as free functions over the frozen `AuthorityState` ports: `admit` (running phase, player capacity, duplicate live identity refusal, monotonic nonzero key), `submit` (control plane Chat/KeepAliveReply to `ControlAccepted` with zero queued commands and no arrival consumed; sequenced intake to `QueuedForTick`), `close_session` (retire-once `StaleSession` semantics), and `apply_sorted_batch` (eligible freeze, in-place domain ordering with `CommandOrderScratch`, watermark application through `apply_sequence`, silent stale-duplicate discard, carry-back without loss on a sort refusal). Deferred minors recorded for the final branch review: the scratch-reservation `Capacity` payload reports the requested batch size as `limit`; the defensive carry-on-sort-failure path is unreachable through public intake and untested; `ServerEndpoint` is not yet implemented as a trait object surface (later adapter nodes own that wiring).

## Task 1.4 bounded mailboxes and cancellation, 2026-09-28

Subagent execution (implementer plus independent reviewer) over the accepted session node. `mornlea_server/src/core/mailbox.rs` now owns the tick and chunk mailbox semantics over the frozen state ports: `budgeted_batch` freezes the eligible batch, orders it in place with the domain comparator, takes a positional prefix of at most the tick command budget, carries the suffix back with unchanged earliest-tick/session/sequence/arrival identity, and returns only the envelopes whose watermark application succeeded — a stale duplicate inside a walked prefix is dropped, never a success and never re-carried. `admit_chunk_result` classifies one chunk completion as `Admitted`, `CancelledDiscarded` or `DuplicateDiscarded` by diffing the new `chunk_discard_counts()` around the state port call, returning the owned record on a capacity refusal; `cancel_chunk_request` arms the one-shot tombstone.

Controller-ratified deviation: the authorized duplicate predicate (queued-results scan) cannot satisfy the pinned late-completion semantics — after a cancellation discard nothing is queued, so a second late completion would be admitted and installed. `state.rs` gained a third private field `chunk_consumed_cancels: BTreeSet<ChunkRequestId>` so a repeat of an already-cancel-discarded request classifies as duplicate; the public surface stayed exactly the authorized getter and signatures, and capacity/push/drain/cancel paths are unchanged. The scoped state edit adds only the two discard counters, that set, the duplicate branch ahead of the capacity check, and the getter.

Behavioral red: the five mailbox cases failed to compile against the reserved module (twelve E0432/E0425/E0599); green: `cargo test -p mornlea_server --test server_contract --locked mailbox` executes five tests — zero-and-prefix-carry proving tick-7 identity through carries with all three envelopes' triples observed (controller added the direct `(9,1,7)` duplicate inspection before the final walk), the budget 4096/4097 ceiling, `late_chunk_once` with counts exactly (1,1) and an empty drain, the queued-duplicate discard, and the global-queue saturation rejecting both sessions with intact watermarks and a controller-added freeze-recall proving all 4096 accepted records survive.

Reviewer verdict approve-with-minors; controller integration applied the two strengthening assertions and records the deferred minors for the final branch review: (c) a completion repeated after its predecessor was drained is currently re-admitted — carried into the chunk-acquisition node brief where stale-generation rejection at install is owned; (d) `chunk_consumed_cancels` is unbounded like the pre-existing tombstone set; (e) the local `sort_batch` duplicates the session provider's helper and repeats the scratch-failure `Capacity` payload naming quirk; (f) `TickCounters.stale`/`carried` stay unwired until the serial tick reducer publishes real counters.

Validation from the worktree, all exit 0: full crate `cargo test -p mornlea_server --locked` (21 passed including all session tests, proving the state edit regressed nothing); `cargo clippy --locked -p mornlea_server --all-targets -- -D warnings`; `cargo fmt -p mornlea_server -- --check`; `go test ./packages/server/sim/runtime -run 'EngineStepPhase|Fluid' -count=1` passed. `git status` shows exactly the provider, its test module and the scoped state edit. Third node of the second three-worker wave (isolated worktree off `a93b2187`; Superpowers implementer on the most capable tier plus independent reviewer, one fix round). ## Agent HTTP test-peer race repair, 2026-09-29

The independent Sol investigator reproduced Darwin accepted-socket inheritance
with a disposable delayed-client probe. The nonblocking test listener's accepted
stream returned WouldBlock even with a read timeout; the helper ignored the read
failure, wrote its scripted response, and closed with the request unread. This
explains the recorded intermittent acquire 503. Controller added
`agent_http::accepted_connection_waits_for_request`: before any request is sent,
the old helper already returns response data (`peek` Ok(1)), a behavioral RED.
The test-only peer now explicitly restores blocking mode, checks the read timeout,
and bounds writes by five seconds. Production HTTP/retry semantics are unchanged.
This repair belongs to the existing Agent wire validation, not a new capability.

cross-task ownership rule.
